  command: "create"
  path: "/app/repo/src/tariff/report.py"
  file_text: 
    """Invoice assembly, statements and rendering for the tariff engine."""
    import csv
    import json
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    from fractions import Fraction
    from pathlib import Path
    
    from . import core, parser
    
    INPUT_FILES = ("meter_reads.jsonl", "accounts.csv", "billing_periods.csv",
                   "rate_card.json")
    
    
    def missing_input(data_dir):
        """The name of the first required input file the directory lacks."""
        root = Path(data_dir)
        for name in INPUT_FILES:
            if not (root / name).is_file():
                return name
        return None
    
    
    def _rows(path):
        with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            return [{key.strip(): (value or "").strip() for key, value in 
row.items()}
                    for row in csv.DictReader(handle)]
    
    
    def _plan_index(plans):
        """Map every plan name and plan code to the name of its plan."""
        index = {}
        for name, plan in plans.items():
            for code in plan.get("codes") or ():
                index.setdefault(str(code).strip(), name)
        index.update({name: name for name in plans})
        return index
    
    
    def _load(data_dir):
        """Read the input files of ``data_dir`` into the engine's own values."""
        root = Path(data_dir)
        reads = parser.parse_reads((root / 
"meter_reads.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        meters = {}
        for row in _rows(root / "accounts.csv"):
            end = date.fromisoformat(row["active_to"]) if row["active_to"] else 
None
            meters.setdefault((row["account"], row["meter"]), []).append(
                (date.fromisoformat(row["active_from"]), end))
        periods = []
        for row in _rows(root / "billing_periods.csv"):
            periods.append({
                "account": row["account"],
                "start": date.fromisoformat(row["start"]),
                "end": date.fromisoformat(row["end"]),
                "plan": row["plan"],
                "commitment": core.round_cents(row["commitment"]),
                "allowance": core.exact(row["allowance"]),
            })
        card = json.loads((root / "rate_card.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        plans = {str(name).strip(): plan for name, plan in 
card["plans"].items()}
        payments = {}
        if (root / "payments.csv").is_file():
            for row in _rows(root / "payments.csv"):
                key = (row["account"], date.fromisoformat(row["start"]))
                payments[key] = payments.get(key, 0) + 
core.round_cents(row["paid"])
        vouchers = {}
        if (root / "vouchers.csv").is_file():
            for row in _rows(root / "vouchers.csv"):
                vouchers.setdefault(row["account"], {})[row["voucher"]] = \
                    core.round_cents(row["amount"])
        return {"reads": reads, "meters": meters, "periods": periods, "plans": 
plans,
                "codes": _plan_index(plans), "payments": payments, "vouchers": 
vouchers}
    
    
    def _active_days(windows, start, end):
        """The days from ``start`` to ``end`` on which a meter is active."""
        days = []
        day = start
        while day <= end:
            if any(opened <= day and (closed is None or day <= closed)
                   for opened, closed in windows):
                days.append(day)
            day += timedelta(days=1)
        return days
    
    
    def _prepare(data):
        """Classify the reads and sum the period quantity of every invoice 
meter."""
        rows = {}
        for row in data["periods"]:
            rows.setdefault(row["account"], []).append(row)
        for account in rows:
            rows[account].sort(key=lambda row: row["start"])
        windows = {account: sorted((row["start"], row["end"]) for row in 
account_rows)
                   for account, account_rows in rows.items()}
        accepted, exceptions = parser.classify(data["reads"], data["meters"], 
windows)
        billed = parser.billed_quantities(accepted)
        usage = {}
        for read in accepted:
            for row in rows[read["account"]]:
                if row["start"] <= read["day"] <= row["end"]:
                    key = (read["account"], row["start"], read["meter"])
                    usage[key] = usage.get(key, 0) + billed[read["read_id"]]
                    break
        account_meters = {}
        for account, meter in data["meters"]:
            account_meters.setdefault(account, []).append(meter)
        for account in account_meters:
            account_meters[account].sort()
        return {"rows": rows, "usage": usage, "meters": account_meters,
                "exceptions": exceptions}
    
    
    def _invoice(data, state, row, tiers, carry, allow_credit):
        """Rate one row of billing_periods.csv into an invoice."""
        account = row["account"]
        plan = data["plans"][data["codes"][row["plan"]]]
        lines = []
        offset = 0
        for meter in state["meters"].get(account, ()):
            quantity = state["usage"].get((account, row["start"], meter), 0)
            lines.extend(core.energy_lines(meter, offset, quantity, tiers))
            offset += quantity
            days = _active_days(data["meters"][(account, meter)], row["start"], 
row["end"])
            if days:
                lines.append(core.standing_line(meter, plan, days))
        energy = [line for line in lines if line["kind"] == "energy"]
        shares, unshared = core.share_allowance([line["exact"] for line in 
energy],
                                                row["allowance"] + carry)
        for line, share in zip(energy, shares):
            line["share"] = share
        amounts = [core.round_cents(line["exact"] - line.get("share", 0))
                   for line in lines]
        subtotal, credit, total = core.settle(amounts, row["commitment"],
                                              plan["credit_rate"], allow_credit)
        return {
            "account": account,
            "start": row["start"],
            "end": row["end"],
            "plan": row["plan"],
            "plan_name": data["codes"][row["plan"]],
            "lines": [{"meter": line["meter"], "kind": line["kind"], "tier": 
line["tier"],
                       "units": line["units"], "amount": core.money(amount)}
                      for line, amount in zip(lines, amounts)],
            "subtotal": subtotal,
            "credit": credit,
            "total": total,
            "paid": data["payments"].get((account, row["start"])),
            "unshared": unshared,
        }
    
    
    def _rate(data, state, tiers_by_plan):
        """Rate every row of billing_periods.csv, in account and then start 
order."""
        invoices = []
        for account in sorted(state["rows"]):
            carry = Fraction(0)
            previous = None
            for row in state["rows"][account]:
                allow_credit = not (previous is not None and previous["paid"] is
not None
                                    and previous["paid"] < previous["total"])
                invoice = _invoice(data, state, row,
                                   tiers_by_plan[data["codes"][row["plan"]]],
                                   carry, allow_credit)
                carry = invoice["unshared"]
                previous = invoice
                invoices.append(invoice)
        return invoices
    
    
    def _fits(invoices, name):
        """Whether every paid invoice on the plan ``name`` totals its paid 
amount."""
        return all(invoice["total"] == invoice["paid"] for invoice in invoices
                   if invoice["plan_name"] == name and invoice["paid"] is not 
None)
    
    
    def _choose_tiers(data, state):
        """The tiers every plan's invoices are rated with.
    
        A plan is rated with its own tiers unless exactly one of its revisions
        makes every paid invoice on the plan total its paid amount.
        """
        choice = {name: plan["tiers"] for name, plan in data["plans"].items()}
        revised = {name: plan["revisions"] for name, plan in 
data["plans"].items()
                   if plan.get("revisions")}
        for _ in range(len(revised) + 1):
            changed = False
            for name, revisions in revised.items():
                fits = []
                for revision in revisions:
                    trial = dict(choice, **{name: revision["tiers"]})
                    if _fits(_rate(data, state, trial), name):
                        fits.append(revision["tiers"])
                picked = fits[0] if len(fits) == 1 else 
data["plans"][name]["tiers"]
                if picked != choice[name]:
                    choice[name] = picked
                    changed = True
            if not changed:
                break
        return choice
    
    
    def _document(data_dir):
        data = _load(data_dir)
        state = _prepare(data)
        invoices = _rate(data, state, _choose_tiers(data, state))
        return data, invoices, state["exceptions"]
    
    
    def build_invoices(data_dir):
        """Rate every billing period of ``data_dir`` and return the invoice 
document."""
        _, invoices, exceptions = _document(data_dir)
        return {
            "invoices": [{
                "account": invoice["account"],
                "period": {"start": invoice["start"].isoformat(),
                           "end": invoice["end"].isoformat()},
                "plan": invoice["plan"],
                "lines": invoice["lines"],
                "subtotal": core.money(invoice["subtotal"]),
                "credit": core.money(invoice["credit"]),
                "total": core.money(invoice["total"]),
            } for invoice in invoices],
            "exceptions": exceptions,
        }
    
    
    def build_statements(data_dir):
        """Return the voucher statement document of ``data_dir``."""
        data, invoices, _ = _document(data_dir)
        balances = {}
        for invoice in invoices:
            balances[invoice["account"]] = (balances.get(invoice["account"], 0)
                                            + invoice["total"] - 
(invoice["paid"] or 0))
        statements = []
        for account in sorted(data["vouchers"]):
            balance = max(balances.get(account, 0), 0)
            chosen, taken = core.redeem(balance, data["vouchers"][account])
            statements.append({"account": account, "balance": 
core.money(balance),
                               "redeemed": chosen, "due": core.money(balance - 
taken)})
        return {"statements": statements}
    
    
    def render(document):
        """Return a document as JSON text."""
        return json.dumps(document, indent=2, sort_keys=True)

Summary: Remove init and main for rewrite

Reasoning:

        document = build_statements(data_dir) if statements else 
build_invoices(data_dir)
        print(render(document))
        return 0

        raise SystemExit(main())

# tariff

`tariff` rates the meter reads of a billing run into customer invoices, using
the Python standard library only.

    PYTHONPATH=src python3 -m tariff /path/to/data

The data directory holds `meter_reads.jsonl`, `accounts.csv`,
`billing_periods.csv` and `rate_card.json`, and optionally `payments.csv` and
`vouchers.csv`. The command prints one JSON document with an `invoices` list
and an `exceptions` list; with `--statements` it prints the voucher statements
of the accounts in `vouchers.csv` instead.

Each row of `billing_periods.csv` is billed as its own invoice. A meter carries
one energy line per plan tier its units reach, counted from the account's first
unit over its meters, and one standing line prorated day by day over the days
of each day's own calendar month. An invoice whose subtotal reaches its
commitment receives the plan's commitment credit.

Run the unit tests with:

    PYTHONPATH=src python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -v
EOF
echo done

Summary: Replay regression cases against new engine

Reasoning:
All 13 tests pass, so now I want to validate against the regression cases in 
/app/ci that correspond to actual customer claims. I'll build a temporary 
validation script that constructs a test directory for each case—meter reads, 
account rows, a billing period, and the rate card—then compares the computed 
invoice total against the customer's claimed amount.

With 2000 cases this should still run in seconds since each run just reloads a 
small rate card file; I'll treat this as a quick sanity check for now and build 
out a proper report generator afterward.



$ mkdir -p /app/work && cat > /app/work/replay.py <<'EOF'
import json, sys, tempfile, shutil, collections
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
import tariff

CI = Path("/app/ci")
card = (CI / "rate_card.json").read_text()
cases = [json.loads(l) for l in (CI / "regression_cases.jsonl").open()]

def run(case, tmp):
    root = Path(tmp)
    (root / "meter_reads.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r 
in case["reads"]))
    (root / "accounts.csv").write_text("account,meter,active_from,active_to\n" +
"".join(
        f"{case['account']},{m['meter']},{m['active_from']},{m['active_to']}\n" 
for m in case["meters"]))
    (root / "billing_periods.csv").write_text(
        "account,start,end,plan,commitment,allowance\n"
        f"{case['account']},{case['period']['start']},{case['period']['end']},{c
ase['plan']},{case['commitment']},{case['allowance']}\n")
    (root / "rate_card.json").write_text(card)
    doc = tariff.build_invoices(tmp)
    return doc["invoices"][0]["total"]

results = []
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
    for case in cases:
        actual = run(case, tmp)
        results.append((case["name"], case["customer_claim"], actual, 
case["disputed_total"]))
json.dump(results, open("/app/work/replay.json", "w"))
ok = sum(1 for _, e, a, _ in results if e == a)
old = sum(1 for _, e, _, d in results if e == d)
print("match claim:", ok, "/", len(results), " old matched:", old)
diffs = [(n, e, a) for n, e, a, _ in results if e != a]
print(diffs[:15])
EOF

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Analyze mismatch by case feature

Reasoning:
1286 out of 2000 match, leaving 714 mismatches — too many to just be bad 
customer claims, so there's probably a systematic issue I'm missing. I want to 
break down the mismatches by feature: malformed floats, duplicate days, dates 
outside the period, multiple meters, nonzero allowances, inactive accounts, and 
check the match rate within each group.



$ cat > /app/work/analyze.py <<'EOF'
import json, collections
from datetime import date
cases = {json.loads(l)["name"]: json.loads(l) for l in 
open("/app/ci/regression_cases.jsonl")}
res = json.load(open("/app/work/replay.json"))
def feats(c):
    f=set()
    if len(c["meters"])>1: f.add("multimeter")
    if c["allowance"]!="0.00": f.add("allowance")
    if any(isinstance(r["quantity"],float) for r in c["reads"]): 
f.add("floatqty")
    s,e=c["period"]["start"],c["period"]["end"]
    if any(not (s<=r["day"]<=e) for r in c["reads"]): f.add("outside")
    cnt=collections.Counter((r["meter"],r["day"]) for r in c["reads"])
    if any(v>1 for v in cnt.values()): f.add("dupday")
    for r in c["reads"]:
        m=[m for m in c["meters"] if m["meter"]==r["meter"]]
        if not m: f.add("unknown"); continue
        m=m[0]
        if not (m["active_from"]<=r["day"] and (not m["active_to"] or 
r["day"]<=m["active_to"])): f.add("inactive")
    if date.fromisoformat(s).month != date.fromisoformat(e).month: 
f.add("crossmonth")
    if not c["reads"]: f.add("noreads")
    return f
tot=collections.Counter(); bad=collections.Counter()
for name, exp, act, dis in res:
    f=feats(cases[name]) or {"plain"}
    f.add("ALL")
    if not (f-{"ALL","crossmonth"}): f.add("plain")
    for k in f:
        tot[k]+=1
        if exp!=act: bad[k]+=1
for k in sorted(tot, key=lambda k:-tot[k]):
    print(f"{k:12s} total {tot[k]:5d} mismatch {bad[k]:5d}  rate 
{bad[k]/tot[k]:.2%}")
EOF

Summary: Inspect plain mismatching cases

Reasoning:

pointing to bogus customer claims rather than a systematic bug. Notably, all 42 
"noreads" cases match, meaning standing-only invoices and the 
standing-plus-credit logic are correct. Plain cases show a 28.9% mismatch rate, 
so I want to dig into those more closely—single meter, no allowance, all reads 
within period, integer quantities, active status.



$ cat > /app/work/plain.py <<'EOF'
import json, collections
from datetime import date
from fractions import Fraction
cases = {json.loads(l)["name"]: json.loads(l) for l in 
open("/app/ci/regression_cases.jsonl")}
res = json.load(open("/app/work/replay.json"))
card=json.load(open("/app/ci/rate_card.json"))["plans"]
def plain(c):
    s,e=c["period"]["start"],c["period"]["end"]
    if len(c["meters"])!=1 or c["allowance"]!="0.00" or not c["reads"]: return 
False
    if any(isinstance(r["quantity"],float) for r in c["reads"]): return False
    if any(not (s<=r["day"]<=e) for r in c["reads"]): return False
    cnt=collections.Counter((r["meter"],r["day"]) for r in c["reads"])
    if any(v>1 for v in cnt.values()): return False
    m=c["meters"][0]
    if any(r["meter"]!=m["meter"] for r in c["reads"]): return False
    if any(not (m["active_from"]<=r["day"] and (not m["active_to"] or 
r["day"]<=m["active_to"])) for r in c["reads"]): return False
    return True
n=0
for name, exp, act, dis in res:
    c=cases[name]
    if not plain(c) or exp==act: continue
    n+=1
    if n>8: break
    q=sum(r["quantity"] for r in c["reads"])
    p=card[c["plan"]]
    print(name, c["plan"], c["period"], "q=",q, "commit",c["commitment"], 
"claim",exp,"mine",act,"old",dis, p["tiers"], p["standing_charge"], 
p["credit_rate"], c["meters"])
print("plain mismatches:", sum(1 for name,exp,act,_ in res if plain(cases[name])
and exp!=act))

$ cat > /app/work/diffs.py <<'EOF'
import json, collections
res = json.load(open("/app/work/replay.json"))
eq_old=0; other=[]
for name,exp,act,dis in res:
    if exp==act: continue
    if exp==dis: eq_old+=1
    else: other.append((name,exp,act,dis))
print("mismatch", 2000-sum(1 for n,e,a,d in res if e==a), "of which 
claim==old:", eq_old, "other:", len(other))
d=collections.Counter()
for n,e,a,dd in other:
    d[round((float(e)-float(a))*100)]+=1
print("diff cents claim-mine:", d.most_common(20))
print("abs>5c:", sum(v for k,v in d.items() if abs(k)>5), "of", sum(d.values()))
EOF

$ cat > /app/work/cover.py <<'EOF'
import json, collections
from datetime import date
cases = {json.loads(l)["name"]: json.loads(l) for l in 
open("/app/ci/regression_cases.jsonl")}
res = json.load(open("/app/work/replay.json"))
def feats(c):
    f=set(); s,e=c["period"]["start"],c["period"]["end"]
    if len(c["meters"])>1: f.add("multimeter")
    if c["allowance"]!="0.00": f.add("allowance")
    if any(isinstance(r["quantity"],float) for r in c["reads"]): 
f.add("floatqty")
    if any(not (s<=r["day"]<=e) for r in c["reads"]): f.add("outside")
    cnt=collections.Counter((r["meter"],r["day"]) for r in c["reads"])
    if any(v>1 for v in cnt.values()): f.add("dupday")
    for r in c["reads"]:
        m=[x for x in c["meters"] if x["meter"]==r["meter"]]
        if not m: f.add("unknownmeter"); continue
        m=m[0]
        if not (m["active_from"]<=r["day"] and (not m["active_to"] or 
r["day"]<=m["active_to"])): f.add("inactive")
    if date.fromisoformat(s).month!=date.fromisoformat(e).month: 
f.add("crossmonth")
    # multi-tier?
    return f
groups=collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)
for name,exp,act,dis in res:
    if exp==act: g="match"
    elif exp==dis: g="claim=old"
    else: g="offset%+d"%round((float(exp)-float(act))*100)
    for f in feats(cases[name]) or {"plain"}: groups[g][f]+=1
    groups[g]["TOTAL"]+=1
for g in sorted(groups): print(g, dict(groups[g]))
EOF

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/work/contract_checks.py"
  file_text: 
    """Check the repaired package against every worked example in the 
contract."""
    import json
    import os
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import tempfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
    import tariff
    
    SRC = "/app/repo/src"
    RESULTS = []
    
    
    def check(name, got, want):
        ok = got == want
        RESULTS.append((name, ok))
        if not ok:
            print(f"FAIL {name}\n  got  {got!r}\n  want {want!r}")
        else:
            print(f"ok   {name}")
    
    
    def write(root, reads, accounts, periods, card, payments=None, 
vouchers=None):
        root = Path(root)
        (root / "meter_reads.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for
r in reads))
        (root / "accounts.csv").write_text(
            "account,meter,active_from,active_to\n" + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n"
for r in accounts))
        (root / "billing_periods.csv").write_text(
            "account,start,end,plan,commitment,allowance\n" + 
"".join(",".join(r) + "\n" for r in periods))
        (root / "rate_card.json").write_text(json.dumps(card))
        for name, rows, header in (("payments.csv", payments, 
"account,start,paid"),
                                   ("vouchers.csv", vouchers, 
"account,voucher,amount")):
            if rows is not None:
                (root / name).write_text(header + "\n" + "".join(",".join(r) + 
"\n" for r in rows))
        return str(root)
    
    
    def plan(**kwargs):
        base = {"standing_charge": 31.00, "credit_rate": 0.0,
                "tiers": [{"up_to": None, "rate": 1.0}]}
        base.update(kwargs)
        return base
    
    
    def run(**kwargs):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            return tariff.build_invoices(write(tmp, **kwargs))
    
    
    def statements(**kwargs):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            return tariff.build_statements(write(tmp, **kwargs))
    
    
    # 1. standing_changes: 10 x 31.00 / 31 + 10 x 62.00 / 31 = 30.00
    card = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(
        standing_changes=[{"from": "2031-05-11", "standing_charge": 62.00}])}}
    doc = run(reads=[], accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-20")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card)
    check("standing_changes example",
          [(l["units"], l["amount"]) for l in doc["invoices"][0]["lines"]], 
[(20, "30.00")])
    
    # 2. accounts.csv example: inactive read, one standing line of 21 units
    card = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan()}}
    doc = run(reads=[{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-15",
                      "quantity": 5}],
              accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-10"), ("X1", "Y9", 
"2031-05-21", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card)
    check("two active windows", doc["exceptions"],
          [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "reason": "inactive_meter"}])
    check("two active windows standing units",
          [(l["kind"], l["units"]) for l in doc["invoices"][0]["lines"]], 
[("standing", 21)])
    
    # 3. billing_periods example: April invoice first, read counts on its own 
invoice
    doc = run(reads=[{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-04-12",
                      "quantity": 7}],
              accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00"),
                       ("X1", "2031-04-01", "2031-04-30", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card)
    check("period order", [i["period"]["start"] for i in doc["invoices"]],
          ["2031-04-01", "2031-05-01"])
    check("read counts on its own period",
          [[(l["kind"], l["units"]) for l in i["lines"]] for i in 
doc["invoices"]],
          [[("energy", 7), ("standing", 30)], [("standing", 31)]])
    
    # 4. cumulative reads bill 0, 40 and 15
    reads = [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
              "quantity": 900, "cumulative": True},
             {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-20",
              "quantity": 940, "cumulative": True},
             {"read_id": "r3", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-27",
              "quantity": 15, "cumulative": True}]
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card)
    check("cumulative example",
          [(l["kind"], l["units"]) for l in doc["invoices"][0]["lines"]],
          [("energy", 55), ("standing", 31)])
    
    # 5. replaced estimate: the measured read bills 37
    reads = [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-04",
              "quantity": 40, "estimated": True},
             {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-04",
              "quantity": 37}]
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card)
    check("replaced estimate", doc["exceptions"],
          [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "reason": "replaced_estimate"}])
    check("replaced estimate units",
          [(l["kind"], l["units"]) for l in doc["invoices"][0]["lines"]],
          [("energy", 37), ("standing", 31)])
    
    # 6. allowance carry over: 1.80 of April's 5.00 lands on May
    card2 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(standing_charge=0.00,
                                                    tiers=[{"up_to": None, 
"rate": 0.1}])}}
    doc = run(reads=[{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-04-12",
                      "quantity": 32},
                     {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-12",
                      "quantity": 100}],
              accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "2031-04-30", "K", "0.00", "5.00"),
                       ("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "5.00")],
card=card2)
    check("allowance carry", [i["subtotal"] for i in doc["invoices"]], ["0.00", 
"3.20"])
    
    # 7. energy share split over two lines, capped at the exact value
    card3 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(standing_charge=0.00, 
tiers=[
        {"up_to": 10, "rate": 1.0}, {"up_to": None, "rate": 1.0}])}}
    doc = run(reads=[{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
                      "quantity": 10},
                     {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Z9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
                      "quantity": 1}],
              accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", ""), ("X1", "Z9", 
"2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "6.00")],
card=card3)
    check("water filled allowance",
          [(l["meter"], l["tier"], l["units"], l["amount"]) for l in 
doc["invoices"][0]["lines"]],
          [("Y9", 1, 10, "5.00"), ("Z9", 2, 1, "0.00")])
    
    # 8. commitment credit and its suppression by an underpaid previous invoice
    card4 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(standing_charge=0.00, 
credit_rate=0.1,
                                                    tiers=[{"up_to": None, 
"rate": 1.0}])}}
    reads = [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-04-12",
              "quantity": 50},
             {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-12",
              "quantity": 50}]
    accounts = [("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")]
    periods = [("X1", "2031-04-01", "2031-04-30", "K", "10.00", "0.00"),
               ("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "10.00", "0.00")]
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, card=card4)
    check("credit on both invoices", [(i["subtotal"], i["credit"], i["total"]) 
for i in doc["invoices"]],
          [("50.00", "5.00", "45.00"), ("50.00", "5.00", "45.00")])
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, card=card4,
              payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "40.00")])
    check("credit suppressed after short payment",
          [(i["subtotal"], i["credit"], i["total"]) for i in doc["invoices"]],
          [("50.00", "5.00", "45.00"), ("50.00", "0.00", "50.00")])
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, card=card4,
              payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "45.00")])
    check("credit kept after full payment",
          [i["credit"] for i in doc["invoices"]], ["5.00", "5.00"])
    
    # 9. tier revisions: the revision under which the one paid invoice totals 
its payment
    card5 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(
        standing_charge=0.00, tiers=[{"up_to": None, "rate": 1.0}],
        revisions=[{"tiers": [{"up_to": None, "rate": 0.5}]},
                   {"tiers": [{"up_to": None, "rate": 0.24}]}])}}
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, card=card5,
              payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "12.00")])
    check("revision picked by paid invoice", [i["total"] for i in 
doc["invoices"]],
          ["12.00", "12.00"])
    
    # 10. statements: balance, voucher choice and due
    doc = statements(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, 
card=card4,
                     payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "45.00"), ("X1", 
"2031-05-01", "37.00")],
                     vouchers=[("X1", "A1", "1.00"), ("X1", "A2", "3.00"), 
("X1", "A3", "4.00"),
                               ("X1", "A4", "8.00")])
    check("statement example", doc["statements"],
          [{"account": "X1", "balance": "8.00", "redeemed": ["A1", "A2", "A3"], 
"due": "0.00"}])
    doc = statements(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, 
card=card4,
                     payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "45.00"), ("X1", 
"2031-05-01", "44.50")],
                     vouchers=[("X1", "A1", "1.00"), ("X1", "A2", "3.00"), 
("X1", "A3", "4.00"),
                               ("X1", "A4", "8.00")])
    check("statement with no fitting voucher", doc["statements"],
          [{"account": "X1", "balance": "0.50", "redeemed": [], "due": "0.50"}])
    doc = statements(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, 
card=card4,
                     payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "99.00"), ("X1", 
"2031-05-01", "99.00")],
                     vouchers=[("X1", "A9", "1.00"), ("X1", "A10", "1.00")])
    check("statement balance never below zero", doc["statements"],
          [{"account": "X1", "balance": "0.00", "redeemed": [], "due": "0.00"}])
    doc = statements(reads=reads, accounts=accounts, periods=periods, 
card=card4,
                     payments=[("X1", "2031-04-01", "45.00"), ("X1", 
"2031-05-01", "43.00")],
                     vouchers=[("X1", "A9", "1.00"), ("X1", "A10", "1.00")])
    check("voucher ids in plain string order", doc["statements"][0]["redeemed"],
["A10", "A9"])
    doc = statements(reads=[], accounts=accounts, periods=[], card=card4, 
vouchers=None)
    check("no vouchers file", doc, {"statements": []})
    
    # 11. plan codes name a plan
    card6 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(standing_charge=0.00, 
codes=[" C7 "])}}
    doc = run(reads=[{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
                      "quantity": 3}],
              accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-01-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "C7", "0.00", 
"0.00")], card=card6)
    check("plan code", (doc["invoices"][0]["plan"], 
doc["invoices"][0]["total"]), ("C7", "3.00"))
    
    # 12. exception order and the first reason that applies
    card7 = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": {"K": plan(standing_charge=0.00)}}
    reads = [{"read_id": "r9", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02", "quantity": 1},
             {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02", "quantity": 1},
             {"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-06-02", "quantity": 1},
             {"read_id": "r3", "account": "X1", "meter": "ZZ", "day": 
"2031-05-02", "quantity": 1},
             {"read_id": "r4", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-03", "quantity": "7"},
             {"read_id": "r5", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-04", "quantity": 2.0},
             {"read_id": "r6", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-05", "quantity": -1}]
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-05-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card7)
    check("exceptions sorted by read_id",
          [(e["read_id"], e["reason"]) for e in doc["exceptions"]],
          [("r1", "outside_period"), ("r2", "duplicate_read"), ("r3", 
"unknown_meter"),
           ("r4", "malformed_quantity"), ("r5", "malformed_quantity"),
           ("r6", "malformed_quantity")])
    
    # 13. a duplicate of a read that was itself refused is not a duplicate
    reads = [{"read_id": "r1", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
              "quantity": 5, "estimated": True},
             {"read_id": "r2", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
              "quantity": 6, "estimated": True},
             {"read_id": "r3", "account": "X1", "meter": "Y9", "day": 
"2031-05-02",
              "quantity": 7}]
    doc = run(reads=reads, accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-05-01", "")],
              periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", "0.00")],
card=card7)
    check("estimates refused for the measured read",
          [(e["read_id"], e["reason"]) for e in doc["exceptions"]],
          [("r1", "replaced_estimate"), ("r2", "replaced_estimate")])
    check("measured read billed", doc["invoices"][0]["lines"],
          [{"meter": "Y9", "kind": "energy", "tier": 1, "units": 7, "amount": 
"7.00"}])
    
    # 14. the command line
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        data = write(tmp, reads=reads, accounts=[("X1", "Y9", "2031-05-01", 
"")],
                     periods=[("X1", "2031-05-01", "2031-05-31", "K", "0.00", 
"0.00")], card=card7,
                     vouchers=[("X1", "A1", "1.00")])
        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=SRC)
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "tariff", data], 
capture_output=True,
                              text=True, env=env)
        check("cli exit", done.returncode, 0)
        check("cli document", json.loads(done.stdout), 
tariff.build_invoices(data))
        check("cli newline", done.stdout.endswith("\n"), True)
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "tariff", "--statements", 
data],
                              capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
        check("cli statements", (done.returncode, json.loads(done.stdout)),
              (0, tariff.build_statements(data)))
        for name in ("rate_card.json", "billing_periods.csv", "accounts.csv", 
"meter_reads.jsonl"):
            os.remove(Path(data) / name)
            done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "tariff", data], 
capture_output=True,
                                  text=True, env=env)
            check(f"cli missing {name}", (done.returncode, done.stdout, 
done.stderr),
                  (2, "", f"missing input: {name}\n"))
            done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "tariff", 
"--statements", data],
                                  capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
            check(f"cli statements missing {name}", (done.returncode, 
done.stdout, done.stderr),
                  (2, "", f"missing input: {name}\n"))
    
    # 15. the package itself
    check("VERSION", tariff.VERSION,
          json.load(open("/app/ci/package_manifest.json"))["version"])
    check("render is json text", json.loads(tariff.render({"invoices": [], 
"exceptions": []})),
          {"invoices": [], "exceptions": []})
    
    # 16. the sample directory of /app/ci
    doc = tariff.build_invoices("/app/ci")
    check("ci determinism", tariff.build_invoices("/app/ci") == doc, True)
    check("ci invoice count", len(doc["invoices"]), 2000)
    check("ci amounts are two decimal strings",
          all(isinstance(value, str) and len(value.split(".")[1]) == 2
              for invoice in doc["invoices"]
              for value in [invoice["subtotal"], invoice["credit"], 
invoice["total"]]
              + [line["amount"] for line in invoice["lines"]]), True)
    check("ci invoices sorted",
          [(i["account"], i["period"]["start"]) for i in doc["invoices"]]
          == sorted((i["account"], i["period"]["start"]) for i in 
doc["invoices"]), True)
    check("ci exceptions sorted by read_id",
          [e["read_id"] for e in doc["exceptions"]] == sorted(e["read_id"] for e
in doc["exceptions"]),
          True)
    check("ci subtotal is the sum of its lines",
          all(round(sum(float(l["amount"]) for l in i["lines"]), 2) == 
float(i["subtotal"])
              for i in doc["invoices"]), True)
    
    print()
    print(f"{sum(1 for _, ok in RESULTS if ok)}/{len(RESULTS)} checks passed")
    json.dump([{"name": n, "passed": ok} for n, ok in RESULTS],
              open("/app/work/contract_checks.json", "w"), indent=1)

vouchers=None):
+26|    root = Path(root)
+27|    (root / "meter_reads.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for
r in reads))

+29|        "account,meter,active_from,active_to\n" + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n"

+31|        "account,start,end,plan,commitment,allowance\n" +

+32|    (root / "rate_card.json").write_text(json.dumps(card))
+33|    for name, rows, header in (("payments.csv", payments, 
"account,start,paid"),
+34|                               ("vouchers.csv", vouchers, 
"account,voucher,amount")):
+35|        if rows is not None:
+36|            (root / name).write_text(header + "\n" + "".join(",".join(r) +

+40|def plan(**kwargs):

Summary: Write deliverables generator script

Reasoning:
The errors are just simulated timeouts, nothing structural, and the whole run 
finishes in about a second. I want to double-check a couple of unvalidated 
design decisions before grading on fresh inputs that might include estimated, 
cumulative, standing_changes, revisions, payments, or vouchers — the energy 
share denominator seems settled, but I still need to revisit the credit rule for
when an account gets no credit on an invoice.

That rule compares against the previous invoice's total, not the current one, 
and this should hold even if the first invoice's own paid-vs-total doesn't 
matter. I'm also confirming the credit suppression chains correctly through 
sequential computation, that `previous` gets updated properly for every row, 
that the revision-fitting check correctly matches paid amounts to totals in 
cents, and that tier selection logic is good enough when trialing other plans. 
Now I'm working through the contract example involving two revisions on plan K 
where the only paid amount comes from a specific invoice.

I'm checking whether `build_invoices` should raise on missing input, deciding 
the CLI already handles that case fine. I also need to verify the repo doesn't 
contain stray `__pycache__` directories before generating the archive and 
deliverables, and I'm weighing whether to add an AGENTS.md file for persistent 
memory — but since the archive must exactly mirror the repaired repo's files, 
adding anything extra feels risky, so I'd rather place it outside the repo if 
needed.

Now I'm drafting the deliverables script and the per-module fix descriptions for
the changelog — noting that tier boundaries are now inclusive rather than 
exclusive, and that tiers accumulate across an account's meters instead of 
resetting at each meter.

Exceptions now sort only by read_id and apply solely to accepted reads, with 
estimated/cumulative flags tracked on reads and cumulative deltas computed for 
billed quantities, plus support for multiple active windows per meter. The 
reporting layer shifts to one invoice per billing_periods row rather than per 
account, assigns reads by date into the right row, bills every meter with lines 
sorted by meter/tier/standing, computes active days per period and window, 
resolves plans and tier revisions, supports payments/vouchers CSVs, and renders 
amounts as two-decimal strings, alongside package metadata fixes.

For the repair report, I'm planning to include the 43 contract checks plus 
visible test pass/fail and regression replay results, actually running the test 
suite via subprocess and recording outcomes, along with stdlib-only import 
verification. I'm writing a script that parses each Python file's AST to collect
top-level imported module names and checks them against sys.stdlib_module_names 
to confirm the package has no non-stdlib dependencies.

Now I'm moving on to writing make_deliverables.py, structuring test_fixes.json 
with per-case regression results (name/expected/actual/passed) and a sorted list
of previously failing cases pulled from the log.

Now the deliverables generator:

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/work/make_deliverables.py"
  file_text: 
    """Build the seven repair deliverables under /app/out."""
    import ast
    import json
    import re
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import tempfile
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
    import tariff
    
    CI = Path("/app/ci")
    REPO = Path("/app/repo")
    OUT = Path("/app/out")
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    BUILD = json.loads((CI / "build_config.json").read_text())
    MANIFEST = json.loads((CI / "package_manifest.json").read_text())
    LINT = json.loads((CI / "lint_report.json").read_text())
    
    CHECKS = []
    
    
    def check(name, passed):
        CHECKS.append({"name": name, "passed": bool(passed)})
        return passed
    
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- the 
archive
    def repo_files():
        return sorted(
            str(path.relative_to(REPO))
            for path in REPO.rglob("*")
            if path.is_file() and "__pycache__" not in path.parts and 
path.suffix != ".pyc"
        )
    
    
    def build_archive():
        archive = BUILD["archive"]
        compression = (zipfile.ZIP_STORED if archive["compression"] == "stored"
                       else zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)
        mode = int(archive["mode"], 8)
        stamp = tuple(archive["timestamp"])
        target = Path(archive["path"])
        with zipfile.ZipFile(target, "w", compression) as bundle:
            for name in repo_files():
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name, date_time=stamp)
                info.compress_type = compression
                info.external_attr = mode << 16
                bundle.writestr(info, (REPO / name).read_bytes())
        with zipfile.ZipFile(target) as bundle:
            infos = bundle.infolist()
            check("archive holds every repository file",
                  [info.filename for info in infos] == repo_files())
            check("archive members sorted by name",
                  [info.filename for info in infos] == sorted(i.filename for i 
in infos))
            check("archive has no directory entry and no cache file",
                  not any(info.is_dir() or "__pycache__" in info.filename
                          or info.filename.endswith(".pyc") for info in infos))
            check("archive timestamps follow build_config",
                  all(info.date_time == stamp for info in infos))
            check("archive compression follows build_config",
                  all(info.compress_type == compression for info in infos))
            check("archive permission bits follow build_config",
                  all(info.external_attr >> 16 == mode for info in infos))
            check("archive code runs", _archive_runs(bundle))
        return target
    
    
    def _archive_runs(bundle):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            bundle.extractall(tmp)
            done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "tariff", str(CI)],
                                  capture_output=True, text=True,
                                  env={"PYTHONPATH": str(Path(tmp) / "src"), 
"PATH": "/usr/bin"})
            if done.returncode != 0:
                return False
            return json.loads(done.stdout) == tariff.build_invoices(str(CI))
    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------- the test 
suite
    def run_visible_tests():
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "discover", 
"-s", "tests", "-v"],
                              cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True,
                              env={"PYTHONPATH": "src", "PATH": "/usr/bin"})
        return done.returncode == 0, done.stderr
    
    
    def visible_test_names(text):
        return sorted(set(re.findall(r"^\s*def (test_\w+)", text, 
re.MULTILINE)))
    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------- the replays
    def replay_cases():
        card = (CI / "rate_card.json").read_text()
        cases = [json.loads(line) for line in (CI / 
"regression_cases.jsonl").open()]
        rows = []
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            for case in cases:
                (root / "meter_reads.jsonl").write_text(
                    "".join(json.dumps(read) + "\n" for read in case["reads"]))
                (root / "accounts.csv").write_text(
                    "account,meter,active_from,active_to\n" + "".join(
                        f"{case['account']},{meter['meter']},{meter['active_from
']},"
                        f"{meter['active_to']}\n" for meter in case["meters"]))
                (root / "billing_periods.csv").write_text(
                    "account,start,end,plan,commitment,allowance\n"
                    f"{case['account']},{case['period']['start']},{case['period'
]['end']},"
                    f"{case['plan']},{case['commitment']},{case['allowance']}\n"
)
                (root / "rate_card.json").write_text(card)
                actual = tariff.build_invoices(tmp)["invoices"][0]["total"]
                rows.append({"name": case["name"], "expected": 
case["customer_claim"],
                             "actual": actual, "passed": actual == 
case["customer_claim"]})
        return rows
    
    
    def previously_failing():
        text = (CI / "failing_tests.log").read_text()
        names = set(re.findall(r"^(?:FAIL|ERROR): (test_\w+)", text, 
re.MULTILINE))
        names |= set(re.findall(r"^(test_\w+) \(.*\) \.\.\. (?:FAIL|ERROR)$", 
text, re.MULTILINE))
        return sorted(name[len("test_"):] for name in names)
    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------- the reports
    def lint_errors(module):
        return sum(1 for issue in LINT["issues"]
                   if issue["module"] == f"tariff.{module}" and 
issue["severity"] == "error")
    
    
    def imported_modules():
        names = set()
        for path in sorted((REPO / "src").rglob("*.py")):
            for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text())):
                if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                    names.update(alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in 
node.names)
                elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.level == 0:
                    names.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
        return names
    
    
    FIXES = {
        "core": [
            "A tier now covers the account positions up to and including its 
up_to; it used to"
            " stop one unit short of it.",
            "Units are rated from the position they hold among the account's 
units over all its"
            " meters instead of restarting the tiers at every meter.",
            "A standing line is now the sum, over the meter's active days, of 
the standing charge"
            " of the day divided by the days of that day's own month, in place 
of a flat 30-day"
            " month.",
            "The standing charge of a day follows the plan's standing_changes 
entry with the"
            " latest from date on or before that day.",
            "Every amount is computed from the decimal values as written in the 
input files with"
            " exact fractions and rounded half to even to 0.01, instead of 
binary floats.",
            "Added the allowance sharing of the energy lines: equal parts capped
at a line's exact"
            " value, re-shared while some stays unshared, and the remainder 
reported back.",
            "The commitment credit is now measured on the invoice subtotal 
rather than on a second"
            " base that also rated refused reads, the total is the discounted 
subtotal rounded half"
            " to even and the credit is what the total takes off the subtotal.",
            "Added the voucher redemption: the largest voucher sum not above the
balance, taking"
            " the set whose id list comes first.",
        ],
        "parser": [
            "A quantity is a usable quantity only when it is a JSON integer of 0
or more, so text,"
            " decimals, booleans and null now receive malformed_quantity.",
            "Added the missing outside_period exception for a read whose day 
lies in no billing"
            " period of its account.",
            "Added the missing replaced_estimate exception for an estimate whose
day already has an"
            " accepted measured read.",
            "duplicate_read now compares the estimated flag as well and only 
fires against an"
            " earlier read that was itself accepted.",
            "The exception list is sorted by read_id alone; it used to be sorted
by account first.",
            "Reads now carry their estimated and cumulative flags, and 
billed_quantities bills a"
            " cumulative read as the rise over the previous accepted cumulative 
read of its meter.",
            "A meter can hold several active windows for one account, so 
inactive_meter looks at"
            " every row of accounts.csv for the pair.",
        ],
        "report": [
            "Every row of billing_periods.csv is billed as its own invoice, 
sorted by account and"
            " then by start; the engine used to keep one invoice per account and
silently drop the"
            " account's other rows.",
            "A read is counted on the invoice whose period holds its day, and 
the period"
            " quantities, positions and active days of an invoice come from that
row alone.",
            "Active days are counted day by day inside the period over the 
meter's own windows,"
            " which also fixes periods that span two months.",
            "The plan of a row is found by plan name or by one of the plan's 
codes.",
            "Added the tier revision choice: a plan is rated with the one 
revision under which"
            " every paid invoice on the plan totals its paid amount.",
            "The allowance that no energy line can take is carried to the 
account's next invoice by"
            " start and dropped after the last one.",
            "An invoice gets no credit when the account's previous invoice by 
start was paid less"
            " than its total, which needed the optional payments.csv.",
            "Added the statement document built from vouchers.csv: balance, 
redeemed voucher ids"
            " and what is due.",
            "Every amount in the document is written as a string with exactly 
two decimals.",
            "Added missing_input so the command line can name the first input 
file a directory"
            " lacks.",
        ],
        "package": [
            "tariff.VERSION, the pyproject version and requires-python now 
follow"
            " package_manifest.json.",
            "The requests dependency is gone: pyproject declares no dependency 
and the package"
            " imports the standard library only.",
            "python -m tariff now accepts --statements, exits 2 and writes 
'missing input: <file>'"
            " on standard error when an input file is missing.",
            "The README no longer describes the 30-day proration and names the 
optional input"
            " files and the statement run.",
            "The two visible tests that asserted the 30-day proration and a 
credit below the"
            " commitment now assert what the contract says.",
        ],
    }
    
    VALIDATIONS = [
        "rate_tiers covers the account positions 1 to up_to of tier 1 and the 
positions above the"
        " previous up_to for every later tier",
        "rate_tiers carries the units of a meter on from the account positions 
of the meters"
        " before it",
        "standing_line prorates 10 days at 31.00 and 10 days at 62.00 over a 
31-day month to 30.00,"
        " the contract's standing_changes example",
        "standing_line prorates 14 February days of an 18.60 charge to 9.30",
        "share_allowance splits 6.00 over lines of 5.00 and 1.00 exact value as 
5.00 and 1.00",
        "share_allowance reports the part no energy line can take so the invoice
can carry it on",
        "settle gives no credit below the commitment and credit = subtotal - 
round(subtotal x (1 -"
        " credit_rate)) at or above it",
        "round_cents rounds half to even, so 0.125 becomes 0.12 and 0.135 
becomes 0.14",
        "redeem reproduces the contract's voucher example: A1, A2 and A3 for a 
balance of 8.00",
        "the 2000 replays of /app/ci/regression_cases.jsonl run through core 
without an error",
    ]
    
    
    def main():
        archive = build_archive()
        tests_pass, _ = run_visible_tests()
        check("the visible tests pass against the repaired package", tests_pass)
    
        contract = 
json.loads(Path("/app/work/contract_checks.json").read_text())
        CHECKS.extend(contract)
    
        document = tariff.build_invoices(str(CI))
        check("two runs on /app/ci return equal documents",
              document == tariff.build_invoices(str(CI)))
        check("tariff.VERSION matches the package manifest", tariff.VERSION == 
MANIFEST["version"])
        pyproject = (REPO / "pyproject.toml").read_text()
        check("pyproject carries the manifest version and python, and no 
dependency",
              f'version = "{MANIFEST["version"]}"' in pyproject
              and f'requires-python = "{MANIFEST["requires_python"]}"' in 
pyproject
              and "dependencies = []" in pyproject)
        modules = imported_modules()
        stdlib_only = all(name in sys.stdlib_module_names or name == "tariff"
                          for name in modules)
        check("the package imports the standard library only", stdlib_only)
    
        rows = replay_cases()
        check("every dispute replay produces an invoice total",
              all(re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.\d\d", row["actual"]) for row in rows))
    
        visible = (REPO / "tests" / "test_visible.py").read_text()
        corrected = ["test_commitment_credit_base", 
"test_partial_period_standing_charge"]
        names = visible_test_names(visible)
        check("no visible test function was deleted or renamed",
              names == sorted(["test_single_tier_energy_line", 
"test_standing_line_full_month",
                               "test_bool_quantity_is_rejected", 
"test_decimal_quantity_is_rejected",
                               "test_unknown_meter_is_reported", 
"test_read_on_inactive_day",
                               "test_duplicate_read_keeps_first", 
"test_padded_identifiers_match",
                               "test_no_credit_below_commitment", 
"test_render_round_trips",
                               "test_cli_prints_document", 
"test_partial_period_standing_charge",
                               "test_commitment_credit_base"]))
        check("no visible test is skipped", "skip" not in visible)
    
        (OUT / "repair-report.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "package": "tariff",
            "fixes": FIXES["package"] + FIXES["core"] + FIXES["parser"] + 
FIXES["report"],
            "checks": CHECKS,
        }, indent=2) + "\n")
    
        (OUT / "module_core.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "module": "core",
            "status": "fixed",
            "fixes": FIXES["core"],
            "lint_errors": lint_errors("core"),
            "validations": VALIDATIONS,
        }, indent=2) + "\n")
    
        (OUT / "module_parser.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "module": "parser",
            "status": "fixed",
            "fixes": FIXES["parser"],
            "lint_errors": lint_errors("parser"),
            "contract_terms": sorted(["VERSION", "build_invoices", "render"]),
        }, indent=2) + "\n")
    
        (OUT / "module_report.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "module": "report",
            "status": "fixed",
            "fixes": FIXES["report"],
            "lint_errors": lint_errors("report"),

+40|def build_archive():
+41|    archive = BUILD["archive"]
+42|    compression = (zipfile.ZIP_STORED if archive["compression"] == "stored"
+43|                   else zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)
+44|    mode = int(archive["mode"], 8)
+45|    stamp = tuple(archive["timestamp"])
+46|    target = Path(archive["path"])
+47|    with zipfile.ZipFile(target, "w", compression) as bundle:
+48|        for name in repo_files():
+49|            info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name, date_time=stamp)
+50|            info.compress_type = compression
+51|            info.external_attr = mode << 16
+52|            bundle.writestr(info, (REPO / name).read_bytes())
+53|    with zipfile.ZipFile(target) as bundle:
+54|        infos = bundle.infolist()
+55|        check("archive holds every repository file",
+56|              [info.filename for info in infos] == repo_files())
+57|        check("archive members sorted by name",
+58|              [info.filename for info in infos] == sorted(i.filename for i 
in infos))
+59|        check("archive has no directory entry and no cache file",
+60|              not any(info.is_dir() or "__pycache__" in info.filename
+61|                      or info.filename.endswith(".pyc") for info in infos))
+62|        check("archive timestamps follow build_config",
+63|              all(info.date_time == stamp for info in infos))
+64|        check("archive compression follows build_config",
+65|              all(info.compress_type == compression for info in infos))
+66|        check("archive permission bits follow build_config",
+67|              all(info.external_attr >> 16 == mode for info in infos))
+68|        check("archive code runs", _archive_runs(bundle))
+69|    return target

Summary: Write direct core module checks

Reasoning:
I actually need to run the validations I claim in module_core.json rather than

that exercises those exact checks and merges its results into CHECKS.

  command: "create"
  path: "/app/work/core_checks.py"
  file_text: 
    """Check the rating, proration and settlement of tariff.core directly."""
    import json
    import sys
    from datetime import date
    from fractions import Fraction
    
    sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
    from tariff import core
    
    RESULTS = []
    
    
    def check(name, got, want):
        ok = got == want
        RESULTS.append({"name": name, "passed": ok})
        print(("ok   " if ok else "FAIL ") + name)
        if not ok:
            print(f"  got  {got!r}\n  want {want!r}")
    
    
    TIERS = [{"up_to": 200, "rate": 0.2}, {"up_to": 600, "rate": 0.1}, {"up_to":
None, "rate": 0.05}]
    
    check("core: tier 1 covers the positions up to and including its up_to",
          core.rate_tiers(0, 200, TIERS), [(1, 200, 0.2)])
    check("core: a later tier starts above the previous up_to",
          core.rate_tiers(0, 201, TIERS), [(1, 200, 0.2), (2, 1, 0.1)])
    check("core: the null tier has no upper end",
          core.rate_tiers(0, 700, TIERS), [(1, 200, 0.2), (2, 400, 0.1), (3, 
100, 0.05)])
    check("core: a meter carries on from the account positions before it",
          core.rate_tiers(150, 100, TIERS), [(1, 50, 0.2), (2, 50, 0.1)])
    check("core: a meter wholly inside one tier has one line",
          core.rate_tiers(300, 100, TIERS), [(2, 100, 0.1)])
    check("core: a meter with no unit has no energy line", core.rate_tiers(10, 
0, TIERS), [])
    
    plan = {"standing_charge": 31.00, "credit_rate": 0.0,
            "standing_changes": [{"from": "2031-05-11", "standing_charge": 
62.00}]}
    days = [date(2031, 5, day) for day in range(1, 21)]
    check("core: the contract's standing_changes example",
          core.round_cents(core.standing_line("Y9", plan, days)["exact"]), 3000)
    check("core: the standing charge of a day before any change",
          core.standing_charge(plan, date(2031, 5, 10)), Fraction(31))
    check("core: the standing charge of a day after a change",
          core.standing_charge(plan, date(2031, 5, 11)), Fraction(62))
    check("core: 14 February days of an 18.60 charge",
          core.round_cents(core.standing_line(
              "M1", {"standing_charge": 18.60},
              [date(2026, 2, day) for day in range(15, 29)])["exact"]), 930)
    
    shares, unshared = core.share_allowance([Fraction(5), Fraction(1)], "6.00")
    check("core: an allowance that fits the lines", (shares, unshared),
          ([Fraction(5), Fraction(1)], Fraction(0)))
    shares, unshared = core.share_allowance([Fraction(5), Fraction(1)], "4.00")
    check("core: an equal part capped at the exact value of a line", (shares, 
unshared),
          ([Fraction(3), Fraction(1)], Fraction(0)))
    shares, unshared = core.share_allowance([Fraction(1)], "5.00")
    check("core: the part no energy line can take stays unshared", (shares, 
unshared),
          ([Fraction(1)], Fraction(4)))
    check("core: no energy line leaves the whole allowance unshared",
          core.share_allowance([], "5.00"), ([], Fraction(5)))
    
    check("core: no credit below the commitment", core.settle([3000, 1000], 
5000, 0.1), (4000, 0, 4000))
    check("core: credit at the commitment", core.settle([3000, 1000], 4000, 
0.1), (4000, 400, 3600))
    check("core: credit rounded half to even", core.settle([1250], 0, 0.1), 
(1250, 112, 1138))
    check("core: a suppressed credit", core.settle([4000], 4000, 0.1, False), 
(4000, 0, 4000))
    
    check("core: round_cents rounds half to even down", 
core.round_cents("0.125"), 12)
    check("core: round_cents rounds half to even up", core.round_cents("0.135"),
14)
    check("core: money writes two decimals", (core.money(0), core.money(5), 
core.money(10437)),
          ("0.00", "0.05", "104.37"))

          core.redeem(800, {"A1": 100, "A2": 300, "A3": 400, "A4": 800}),
          (["A1", "A2", "A3"], 800))
    check("core: no voucher fits the balance", core.redeem(50, {"A1": 100}), 
([], 0))
    check("core: vouchers in plain string order", core.redeem(200, {"A9": 100, 
"A10": 100}),
          (["A10", "A9"], 200))
    check("core: the largest sum not above the balance",
          core.redeem(1000, {"B1": 700, "B2": 400, "B3": 250}), (["B1", "B3"], 
950))
    
    print()
    print(f"{sum(1 for item in RESULTS if item['passed'])}/{len(RESULTS)} core 
checks passed")
    json.dump(RESULTS, open("/app/work/core_checks.json", "w"), indent=1)

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Bound voucher subset-sum bitset by voucher total

Reasoning:
With everything else checking out, I'm doing one more careful pass against the 
contract details to catch any subtle divergence that fresh test inputs might 
expose, starting with the account position rule and energy line tier coverage.

I continue walking through each spec requirement—line ordering, standing line 
conditions, exception sorting, exact invoice and statement key sets, due 
calculation, missing input handling, and timing constraints—confirming each 
matches my implementation. One remaining concern surfaces: if a voucher balance 
were extremely large, a bitset-based approach could blow up memory, though 
realistic input scales should keep this safe.

I realize I can bound the bitset by capping the reachable sum at the minimum of 
the balance and the total voucher amount sum, since any subset sum can't exceed 
that total anyway—this keeps memory proportional to actual voucher totals rather
than the raw balance value. I verify this cap is sound for the greedy redemption
logic too, where checking reachable amounts at `left - amount` stays within the 
same bound, then move to apply this fix and check how windows get sorted in the 
`_prepare` function.

I check edge cases: an account present in one CSV but missing from the other is 
ruled out by the data contract, and I confirm the per-account meter lookups 
always resolve correctly. The standing charge proration divides by each day's 
own month length, which works for single-month periods, so I move to consider 
what happens when a billing period spans across two different months.

Rounding for the settlement total follows the half-to-even rule to 0.01 as 
specified, and zero-subtotal invoices resolve cleanly to zero credit. I also 
verify that booleans are properly rejected in quantity parsing, and flag that 
JSON floats like 1e3 would be malformed since the contract requires strict 
integers.

Checking a few remaining edge cases: `render` can serialize without indentation 
since whitespace formatting is unconstrained, `build_invoices` should accept 
both Path and string inputs, and exporting `build_statements` alongside the 
three specified entry points shouldn't violate the contract. Now I'll apply the 
redeem limit change and rerun the full check.

Summary: Differential test of allowance sharing variants

Reasoning:
I'm confirming the empty-vouchers edge case resolves correctly, then planning to
rerun all checks, regenerate deliverables, clean out __pycache__, and fuzz test

I also want to differentially test my `share_allowance` implementation against a
literal brute-force version of the contract wording that does a first round over
all energy lines, to confirm they're equivalent on random inputs.



$ cd /app/work && python - <<'EOF'
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction
sys.path.insert(0,'/app/repo/src')
from tariff import core

def literal(values, allowance):
    """First round over every energy line, then only over the lines above 
zero."""
    shares=[Fraction(0)]*len(values)
    pool=core.exact(allowance)
    rounds=0
    while pool>0:
        idx=list(range(len(values))) if rounds==0 else [i for i in 
range(len(values)) if values[i]-shares[i]>0]
        if not idx: break
        part=pool/len(idx)
        moved=Fraction(0)
        for i in idx:
            t=min(part, values[i]-shares[i])
            shares[i]+=t; pool-=t; moved+=t
        rounds+=1
        if moved==0 and rounds>0: break
    return shares, pool

random.seed(7)
bad=0
for _ in range(20000):
    n=random.randint(0,5)
    values=[Fraction(random.randint(0,500),100) for _ in range(n)]
    allowance=Fraction(random.randint(0,800),100)
    a=core.share_allowance(values, allowance)
    b=literal(values, allowance)
    if a!=b:
        bad+=1
        if bad<4: print("diff", values, allowance, a, b)
    # invariants
    shares, pool = a
    assert all(0<=s<=v for s,v in zip(shares,values))
    assert sum(shares)+pool==allowance
    assert pool==0 or all(s==v for s,v in zip(shares,values))
print("differences:", bad)
EOF

import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction
sys.path.insert(0,'/app/repo/src')
from tariff import core
def literal(values, allowance):
    """First round over every energy line, then only over the lines above 
zero."""
    shares=[Fraction(0)]*len(values)
    pool=core.exact(allowance)
    rounds=0
    while pool>0:
        idx=list(range(len(values))) if rounds==0 else [i for i in 
range(len(values)) if values[i]-shares[i]>0]
        if not idx: break
        part=pool/len(idx)
        moved=Fraction(0)
        for i in idx:
            t=min(part, values[i]-shares[i])
            shares[i]+=t; pool-=t; moved+=t
        rounds+=1
        if moved==0 and rounds>0: break

    return shares, pool
random.seed(7)
bad=0
for _ in range(20000):
    n=random.randint(0,5)
    values=[Fraction(random.randint(0,500),100) for _ in range(n)]
    allowance=Fraction(random.randint(0,800),100)
    a=core.share_allowance(values, allowance)
    b=literal(values, allowance)
    if a!=b:
        bad+=1
        if bad<4: print("diff", values, allowance, a, b)
    # invariants
    shares, pool = a
    assert all(0<=s<=v for s,v in zip(shares,values))
    assert sum(shares)+pool==allowance
    assert pool==0 or all(s==v for s,v in zip(shares,values))
print("differences:", bad)

Summary: Write randomized invariant fuzz test

Reasoning:
Both interpretations of the allowance rule match across 20000 random test cases,
including zero-value lines. Now I want to broaden the fuzz testing of 
build_invoices to catch crashes on unusual combinations—mixed 
estimated/cumulative values, multiple windows and periods, payments, vouchers, 
revisions, and standing changes—so I'm planning a script that generates random 
directories and verifies invariants against an independent recomputation of 
invoice fields.

A full independent reimplementation would be overkill, so instead I'll check 
structural invariants: every read appears exactly once in exceptions or 
contributes to usage, subtotal matches the sum of line amounts, total/credit 
figures stay consistent, lines remain properly sorted, and statement due amounts
equal balance minus redeemed sums with redeemed forming a maximal subset. I also
want to verify determinism and absence of crashes. Let me get this written.



Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/work/fuzz.py"
  file_text: 
    """Random directories: check the invariants the contract puts on a 
document."""
    import itertools
    import json
    import random
    import sys
    import tempfile
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    from decimal import Decimal
    from pathlib import Path
    
    sys.path.insert(0, "/app/repo/src")
    import tariff
    
    random.seed(11)
    
    
    def money(value):
        return f"{Decimal(value).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))}"
    
    
    def make(root, rng):
        accounts = [f"A{n}" for n in range(1, rng.randint(2, 4))]
        plans = {}
        for name in ("P1", "P2"):
            tiers, cap = [], 0
            for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
                cap += rng.randint(1, 60)
                tiers.append({"up_to": cap, "rate": round(rng.uniform(0, 0.5), 
8)})
            tiers.append({"up_to": None, "rate": round(rng.uniform(0, 0.5), 4)})
            plan = {"standing_charge": round(rng.uniform(0, 40), 2),
                    "credit_rate": round(rng.uniform(0, 0.2), 4), "tiers": 
tiers}
            if rng.random() < 0.4:
                plan["standing_changes"] = [
                    {"from": f"2031-0{rng.randint(4, 6)}-{rng.randint(1, 
28):02d}",
                     "standing_charge": round(rng.uniform(0, 40), 2)}]
            if rng.random() < 0.3:
                plan["codes"] = [f"C{name}"]
            if rng.random() < 0.3:
                plan["revisions"] = [{"tiers": [{"up_to": None, "rate": 
round(rng.uniform(0, 1), 4)}]}
                                     for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 2))]
            plans[name] = plan
        card = {"currency": "EUR", "plans": plans}
        meters, periods, payments, vouchers = [], [], [], []
        for account in accounts:
            for meter in [f"M{n}" for n in range(1, rng.randint(2, 4))]:
                day = date(2031, 3, 1)
                while day < date(2031, 7, 1) and rng.random() < 0.8:
                    opened = day + timedelta(days=rng.randint(0, 40))
                    closed = opened + timedelta(days=rng.randint(0, 40))
                    if rng.random() < 0.3:
                        meters.append((account, meter, opened.isoformat(), ""))
                        break
                    meters.append((account, meter, opened.isoformat(), 
closed.isoformat()))
                    day = closed + timedelta(days=rng.randint(1, 10))
            start = date(2031, 4, 1)
            for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
                end = start + timedelta(days=rng.randint(0, 45))
                name = rng.choice(list(plans))
                label = rng.choice(plans[name].get("codes") or [name])
                periods.append((account, start.isoformat(), end.isoformat(), 
label,
                                money(rng.uniform(0, 60)), money(rng.uniform(0, 
8))))
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    payments.append((account, start.isoformat(), 
money(rng.uniform(0, 60))))
                start = end + timedelta(days=rng.randint(1, 5))
            for n in range(rng.randint(0, 4)):
                vouchers.append((account, f"V{account}{n}", 
money(rng.uniform(0.01, 30))))
        reads = []
        for n in range(rng.randint(0, 40)):
            account = rng.choice(accounts)
            meter = rng.choice([m for m in ("M1", "M2", "M3", "M9")])
            day = date(2031, 4, 1) + timedelta(days=rng.randint(-20, 110))
            quantity = rng.choice([rng.randint(0, 900), rng.randint(0, 900), -3,
2.5, True, None, "7"])
            read = {"read_id": f"R{n:03d}", "account": account, "meter": meter,
                    "day": day.isoformat(), "quantity": quantity}
            if rng.random() < 0.3:
                read["estimated"] = rng.random() < 0.5
            if rng.random() < 0.3:
                read["cumulative"] = rng.random() < 0.5
            reads.append(read)
        root = Path(root)
        (root / "meter_reads.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for
r in reads))
        (root / 
"accounts.csv").write_text("account,meter,active_from,active_to\n"
                                           + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n" for r in
meters))
        (root / "billing_periods.csv").write_text(
            "account,start,end,plan,commitment,allowance\n"
            + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n" for r in periods))
        (root / "rate_card.json").write_text(json.dumps(card))
        for name in ("payments.csv", "vouchers.csv"):
            (root / name).unlink(missing_ok=True)
        if payments and rng.random() < 0.8:
            (root / "payments.csv").write_text("account,start,paid\n"
                                               + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n" for 
r in payments))
        if vouchers and rng.random() < 0.8:
            (root / "vouchers.csv").write_text("account,voucher,amount\n"
                                               + "".join(",".join(r) + "\n" for 
r in vouchers))
        return reads, meters, periods, vouchers, card
    
    
    def cents(text):
        return int(Decimal(text) * 100)
    
    
    def main():
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            for round_number in range(400):
                rng = random.Random(round_number)
                reads, meters, periods, vouchers, card = make(tmp, rng)
                document = tariff.build_invoices(tmp)
                assert document == tariff.build_invoices(tmp), "not 
deterministic"
                assert json.loads(tariff.render(document)) == document
                invoices = document["invoices"]
                assert len(invoices) == len(periods), (len(invoices), 
len(periods))
                assert [(i["account"], i["period"]["start"]) for i in invoices] 
== sorted(
                    (i["account"], i["period"]["start"]) for i in invoices)
                ids = [e["read_id"] for e in document["exceptions"]]
                assert ids == sorted(ids) and len(set(ids)) == len(ids)
                assert set(ids) <= {r["read_id"] for r in reads}
                for exception in document["exceptions"]:
                    assert exception["reason"] in (
                        "malformed_quantity", "unknown_meter", "inactive_meter",
                        "outside_period", "duplicate_read", 
"replaced_estimate"), exception
                for invoice in invoices:
                    lines = invoice["lines"]
                    order = [(line["meter"], line["tier"] if line["kind"] == 
"energy" else 10 ** 6)
                             for line in lines]
                    assert order == sorted(order), order
                    for meter, group in itertools.groupby(lines, key=lambda 
line: line["meter"]):
                        kinds = [line["kind"] for line in group]
                        assert kinds.count("standing") <= 1
                        assert kinds == sorted(kinds, key=lambda k: k == 
"standing")
                    assert all(line["units"] >= 1 for line in lines)
                    assert sum(cents(line["amount"]) for line in lines) == 
cents(invoice["subtotal"])
                    assert cents(invoice["subtotal"]) - cents(invoice["credit"])
== cents(
                        invoice["total"])
                    assert cents(invoice["credit"]) >= 0
                    for value in [invoice["subtotal"], invoice["credit"], 
invoice["total"]] + [
                            line["amount"] for line in lines]:
                        assert len(value.split(".")[1]) == 2, value
                statements = tariff.build_statements(tmp)["statements"]
                holders = sorted({v[0] for v in vouchers}) if (
                    Path(tmp, "vouchers.csv").exists()) else []
                assert [s["account"] for s in statements] == holders, 
(statements, holders)
                for statement in statements:
                    amounts = {v[1]: cents(v[2]) for v in vouchers if v[0] == 
statement["account"]}
                    taken = sum(amounts[v] for v in statement["redeemed"])
                    assert statement["redeemed"] == 
sorted(statement["redeemed"])
                    assert taken <= cents(statement["balance"])
                    assert cents(statement["due"]) == 
cents(statement["balance"]) - taken
                    best = max((sum(c) for n in range(len(amounts) + 1)
                                for c in 
itertools.combinations(amounts.values(), n)
                                if sum(c) <= cents(statement["balance"])), 
default=0)
                    assert taken == best, (statement, amounts)
        print("fuzz ok")
    
    
    main()
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

## What was wrong and what I did

I rewrote the three engine modules against `/app/ci/api_contract.md` (exact 
decimal arithmetic with `Fraction` + half-to-even rounding to the cent, amounts 
written as two-decimal strings), and validated the result with:

- the 13 visible tests (all pass; 2 corrected),
- 43 end-to-end checks reproducing **every worked example in the contract** 
(standing_changes, two active windows, period split, cumulative reads, replaced 
estimates, allowance carry, credit suppression, tier revisions, voucher 
statements, CLI exits),
- 25 direct `core` checks, a 20 000-case differential test of the 
allowance-sharing wording, and a 400-directory randomized invariant fuzz,
- the 2000 dispute replays: 1286 now equal the customer claim, and of the 714 
that differ, 363 are claims that simply repeat the old buggy `disputed_total` 
and the remaining 351 differ from my totals by exactly one of four fixed offsets
(+0.90, +0.25, −0.40, −1.50), i.e. deliberately wrong claims — no residual 
scattered disagreement.

**`tariff.report`** — `missing_input` (new), `_plan_index` (new), `_load`,

exit 2).

exported.
